Compute weighted differences\n    dx = X[:, 0:1] - X[:, 0:1].T  # x differences\n    dy = X[:, 1:2] - X[:, 1:2].T  # y differences\n\n    # Apply weights and compute distance\n    dist = np.sqrt((w * dx)**2 + ((2 - w) * dy)**2)\n    return dist\n\n\ndef greedy_matching(centroids, expert_points, max_dist=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Greedy matching of cluster centroids to expert annotations.\n    Match closest pairs first, with maximum distance constraint.\n    Uses standard Euclidean distance.\n\n    Returns: list of (centroid_idx, expert_idx, distance) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(centroids) == 0 or len(expert_points) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute standard Euclidean distance matrix\n    dist_matrix = cdist(centroids, expert_points, metric='euclidean')\n\n    matches = []\n    used_centroids = set()\n    used_experts = set()\n\n    # Get all distances with indices, sorted by distance\n    distances_with_idx = []\n    for i in range(len(centroids)):\n        for j in range(len(expert_points)):\n            distances_with_idx.append((dist_matrix[i, j], i, j))\n\n    distances_with_idx.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n    for dist, c_idx, e_idx in distances_with_idx:\n        if dist > max_dist:\n            break\n        if c_idx not in used_centroids and e_idx not in used_experts:\n            matches.append((c_idx, e_idx, dist))\n            used_centroids.add(c_idx)\n            used_experts.add(e_idx)\n\n    return matches\n\n\ndef compute_f1_delta_for_image(citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run DBSCAN on citizen science points, compute F1 and delta for one image.\n    Returns (f1, delta) where delta is NaN if no matches.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(citsci_points) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Compute custom distance matrix for DBSCAN\n    dist_matrix = weighted_distance(citsci_points, shape_weight)\n\n    # Run DBSCAN with precomputed distances\n    db = DBSCAN(eps=epsilon, min_samples=min_samples, metric='precomputed')\n    labels = db.fit_predict(dist_matrix)\n\n    # Get unique cluster labels (excluding noise = -1)\n    unique_labels = set(labels)\n    unique_labels.discard(-1)\n\n    if len(unique_labels) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Compute cluster centroids\n    centroids = []\n    for label in unique_labels:\n        mask = labels == label\n        cluster_points = citsci_points[mask]\n        centroid = cluster_points.mean(axis=0)\n        centroids.append(centroid)\n    centroids = np.array(centroids)\n\n    # Greedy matching\n    matches = greedy_matching(centroids, expert_points, max_dist=100)\n\n    if len(matches) == 0:\n        return 0.0, np.nan\n\n    # Compute metrics\n    tp = len(matches)\n    fp = len(centroids) - tp  # Unmatched centroids\n    fn = len(expert_points) - tp  # Unmatched expert points\n\n    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0\n    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0\n    f1 = 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0\n\n    # Average delta (Euclidean distance) for matched pairs\n    delta = np.mean([m[2] for m in matches])\n\n    return f1, delta\n\n\ndef evaluate_hyperparams(params, citsci_df, expert_df, all_images):\n    \"\"\"\n    Evaluate a single hyperparameter combination across all images.\n    \"\"\"\n    min_samples, epsilon, shape_weight = params\n\n    f1_scores = []\n    deltas = []\n\n    for file_rad in all_images:\n        # Get citizen science points for this image\n        citsci_mask = citsci_df['file_rad'] == file_rad\n        citsci_points = citsci_df.loc[citsci_mask, ['x', 'y']].values\n\n        # Get expert points for this image\n        expert_mask = expert_df['file_rad'] == file_rad\n        expert_points = expert_df.loc[expert_mask, ['x', 'y']].values\n\n        if len(expert_points) == 0:\n            # Skip images with no expert annotations\n            continue\n\n        f1, delta = compute_f1_delta_for_image(\n            citsci_points, expert_points, epsilon, min_samples, shape_weight\n        )\n\n        f1_scores.append(f1)\n        if not np.isnan(delta):\n            deltas.append(delta)\n\n    # Compute averages\n    avg_f1 = np.mean(f1_scores) if f1_scores else 0.0\n    avg_delta = np.mean(deltas) if deltas else np.nan\n\n    return {\n        'min_samples': min_samples,\n        'epsilon': epsilon,\n        'shape_weight': shape_weight,\n        'F1': avg_f1,\n        'delta': avg_delta\n    }\n\n\ndef find_pareto_frontier(df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Find Pareto-optimal points: maximize F1, minimize delta.\n    A point is Pareto-optimal if no other point is better in both objectives.\n    \"\"\"\n    pareto_mask = np.ones(len(df), dtype=bool)\n\n    for i in range(len(df)):\n        for j in range(len(df)):\n            if i != j:\n                # Point j dominates point i if:\n                # j has higher or equal F1 AND lower or equal delta\n                # AND at least one is strictly better\n                if (df.iloc[j]['F1'] >= df.iloc[i]['F1'] and\n                    df.iloc[j]['delta'] <= df.iloc[i]['delta'] and\n                    (df.iloc[j]['F1'] > df.iloc[i]['F1'] or\n                     df.iloc[j]['delta'] < df.iloc[i]['delta'])):\n                    pareto_mask[i] = False\n                    break\n\n    return df[pareto_mask]\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading data...\")\n    citsci_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/citsci_train.csv')\n    expert_df = pd.read_csv('/root/data/expert_train.csv')\n\n    print(f\"Citizen science annotations: {len(citsci_df)}\")\n    print(f\"Expert annotations: {len(expert_df)}\")\n\n    # Get all unique images from expert dataset\n    all_images = expert_df['file_rad'].unique()\n    print(f\"Unique images in expert dataset: {len(all_images)}\")\n\n    # Define hyperparameter grid\n    min_samples_range = list(range(3, 10))  # 3-9\n    epsilon_range = list(range(4, 25, 2))   # 4, 6, 8, ..., 24\n    shape_weight_range = [round(0.9 + 0.1 * i, 1) for i in range(11)]  # 0.9, 1.0, ..., 1.9\n\n    # Create all parameter combinations\n    param_combinations = [\n        (ms, eps, sw)\n        for ms in min_samples_range\n        for eps in epsilon_range\n        for sw in shape_weight_range\n    ]\n\n    print(f\"Total hyperparameter combinations: {len(param_combinations)}\")\n    print(f\"min_samples: {min_samples_range}\")\n    print(f\"epsilon: {epsilon_range}\")\n    print(f\"shape_weight: {shape_weight_range}\")\n\n    # Run parallel grid search\n    print(\"\\nRunning parallel grid search...\")\n    results = Parallel(n_jobs=-1, verbose=10)(\n        delayed(evaluate_hyperparams)(params, citsci_df, expert_df, all_images)\n        for params in param_combinations\n    )\n\n    # Convert to DataFrame\n    results_df = pd.DataFrame(results)\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal results: {len(results_df)}\")\n    print(f\"Results with valid delta: {results_df['delta'].notna().sum()}\")\n\n    # Filter results with F1 > 0.5\n    filtered_df = results_df[results_df['F1'] > 0.5].copy()\n    print(f\"Results with F1 > 0.5: {len(filtered_df)}\")\n\n    if len(filtered_df) == 0:\n        print(\"No results with F1 > 0.5 found!\")\n        # Save empty file\n        pd.DataFrame(columns=['F1', 'delta', 'min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight']).to_csv(\n            '/root/pareto_frontier.csv', index=False\n        )\n        return\n\n    # Find Pareto frontier\n    pareto_df = find_pareto_frontier(filtered_df)\n    print(f\"\\nPareto-optimal points: {len(pareto_df)}\")\n\n    # Round values as specified\n    pareto_df = pareto_df.copy()\n    pareto_df['F1'] = pareto_df['F1'].round(5)\n    pareto_df['delta'] = pareto_df['delta'].round(5)\n    pareto_df['shape_weight'] = pareto_df['shape_weight'].round(1)\n    pareto_df['min_samples'] = pareto_df['min_samples'].astype(int)\n    pareto_df['epsilon'] = pareto_df['epsilon'].astype(int)\n\n    # Select and order columns\n    output_df = pareto_df[['F1', 'delta', 'min_samples', 'epsilon', 'shape_weight']]\n\n    # Sort by F1 descending for readability\n    output_df = output_df.sort_values('F1', ascending=False)\n\n    #
